==================== FILE: db-write-queue.js ====================
'use strict';

let activeQueueInstance = null;

function isBenignTxError(err) {
  if (!err) return false;
  const msg = String(err.message || '');
  return (
    msg.includes('no such savepoint') ||
    msg.includes('cannot start a transaction within a transaction') ||
    msg.includes('cannot commit - no transaction is active') ||
    msg.includes('cannot rollback - no transaction is active')
  );
}

function normalizeArgs(params, callback) {
  if (typeof params === 'function') {
    return { params: [], callback: params };
  }
  return {
    params: params === undefined || params === null ? [] : params,
    callback: typeof callback === 'function' ? callback : null
  };
}

class DbWriteQueue {
  constructor(dbGetter) {
    this.getDb = typeof dbGetter === 'function' ? dbGetter : () => dbGetter;
    this.chain = Promise.resolve();
    this.txDepth = 0;
    this.spCounter = 0;
  }

  get db() {
    return this.getDb();
  }

  enqueue(taskFn) {
    if (this.txDepth > 0) {
      return Promise.resolve().then(() => taskFn());
    }
    const next = this.chain.then(() => taskFn());
    this.chain = next.catch(() => {});
    return next;
  }

  runAsync(sql, params = [], callback = null) {
    const norm = normalizeArgs(params, callback);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const database = this.db;
      if (!database) return reject(new Error('Database instance is not initialized'));
      const runner = database._rawRun || database.run.bind(database);

      runner(sql, norm.params, function onRunComplete(err) {
        if (err && !isBenignTxError(err)) {
          if (norm.callback) norm.callback.call(this, err);
          return reject(err);
        }
        const resObj = {
          lastID: this && this.lastID !== undefined ? this.lastID : 0,
          changes: this && this.changes !== undefined ? this.changes : 0
        };
        if (norm.callback) norm.callback.call(resObj, null, resObj);
        resolve(resObj);
      });
    });
  }

  getAsync(sql, params = [], callback = null) {
    const norm = normalizeArgs(params, callback);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const database = this.db;
      if (!database) return reject(new Error('Database instance is not initialized'));
      const getter = database._rawGet || database.get.bind(database);

      getter(sql, norm.params, (err, row) => {
        if (err) {
          if (norm.callback) norm.callback(err);
          return reject(err);
        }
        if (norm.callback) norm.callback(null, row);
        resolve(row);
      });
    });
  }

  allAsync(sql, params = [], callback = null) {
    const norm = normalizeArgs(params, callback);
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const database = this.db;
      if (!database) return reject(new Error('Database instance is not initialized'));
      const aller = database._rawAll || database.all.bind(database);

      aller(sql, norm.params, (err, rows) => {
        if (err) {
          if (norm.callback) norm.callback(err);
          return reject(err);
        }
        const safeRows = rows || [];
        if (norm.callback) norm.callback(null, safeRows);
        resolve(safeRows);
      });
    });
  }

  execAsync(sql, callback = null) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const database = this.db;
      if (!database) return reject(new Error('Database instance is not initialized'));
      const execer = database._rawExec || database.exec.bind(database);

      execer(sql, (err) => {
        if (err && !isBenignTxError(err)) {
          if (typeof callback === 'function') callback(err);
          return reject(err);
        }
        if (typeof callback === 'function') callback(null);
        resolve();
      });
    });
  }

  dbRun(sql, params = [], callback = null) {
    return this.runAsync(sql, params, callback);
  }

  dbGet(sql, params = [], callback = null) {
    return this.getAsync(sql, params, callback);
  }

  dbAll(sql, params = [], callback = null) {
    return this.allAsync(sql, params, callback);
  }

  dbExec(sql, callback = null) {
    return this.execAsync(sql, callback);
  }

  // توليد الرقم التسلسلي التالي للفواتير والمستندات (db.nextNumber) المتوافق مع server.js
  async nextNumber(key = 'default', startAt = 1, callback = null) {
    if (typeof startAt === 'function') {
      callback = startAt;
      startAt = 1;
    }
    const cleanKey = String(key || 'default').trim();
    const settingKey = `seq_${cleanKey}`;

    try {
      // 1. محاولة القراءة من جدول counters أو sequences إن كان موجوداً في قاعدة البيانات
      let storedSeq = 0;
      const counterRow = await this.getAsync(
        'SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key IN (?, ?)',
        [settingKey, cleanKey]
      ).catch(() => null);

      if (counterRow && counterRow.value !== undefined) {
        const parsed = parseInt(String(counterRow.value), 10);
        if (!isNaN(parsed)) storedSeq = parsed;
      }

      // فحص جدول counters المخصص إن وجد
      const dedicatedCounter = await this.getAsync(
        'SELECT seq, value, current_value FROM counters WHERE name = ? OR key = ?',
        [cleanKey, cleanKey]
      ).catch(() => null);
      if (dedicatedCounter) {
        const cVal = parseInt(dedicatedCounter.seq ?? dedicatedCounter.value ?? dedicatedCounter.current_value ?? 0, 10);
        if (!isNaN(cVal)) storedSeq = Math.max(storedSeq, cVal);
      }

      // 2. فحص أعلى رقم فاتورة في جدول sales لضمان عدم تكرار أي رقم فاتورة موجود مسبقاً
      const salesMaxRow = await this.getAsync(
        'SELECT MAX(id) AS maxId, COUNT(*) AS totalCount, MAX(doc_number) AS maxDoc FROM sales'
      ).catch(() => null);

      let maxFromSales = 0;
      if (salesMaxRow) {
        maxFromSales = Math.max(Number(salesMaxRow.maxId || 0), Number(salesMaxRow.totalCount || 0));
        if (salesMaxRow.maxDoc) {
          const tailMatch = String(salesMaxRow.maxDoc).match(/(\d+)\s*$/);
          if (tailMatch && tailMatch[1]) {
            const tailNum = parseInt(tailMatch[1], 10);
            if (!isNaN(tailNum) && tailNum < 10000000) {
              maxFromSales = Math.max(maxFromSales, tailNum);
            }
          }
        }
      }

      const baseValue = Math.max(storedSeq, maxFromSales, (Number(startAt) || 1) - 1);
      const nextVal = baseValue + 1;

      // 3. حفظ الرقم الجديد في جدول settings (وجدول counters إن وجد)
      await this.runAsync(
        'INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value',
        [settingKey, String(nextVal)]
      ).catch(() =>
        this.runAsync('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)', [settingKey, String(nextVal)])
      );

      await this.runAsync(
        'UPDATE counters SET seq = ? WHERE name = ? OR key = ?',
        [nextVal, cleanKey, cleanKey]
      ).catch(() => {});

      if (typeof callback === 'function') callback(null, nextVal);
      return nextVal;
    } catch (err) {
      const fallback = Date.now() % 100000;
      if (typeof callback === 'function') callback(null, fallback);
      return fallback;
    }
  }

  async executeTx(workFn) {
    const runTx = async () => {
      const spName = `sp_gherbi_${++this.spCounter}_${Date.now()}`;
      this.txDepth++;
      try {
        await this.runAsync(`SAVEPOINT ${spName}`);
        const result = await workFn(this.db || this);
        await this.runAsync(`RELEASE SAVEPOINT ${spName}`).catch((err) => {
          if (!isBenignTxError(err)) throw err;
        });
        return result;
      } catch (err) {
        await this.runAsync(`ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT ${spName}`).catch(() => {});
        await this.runAsync(`RELEASE SAVEPOINT ${spName}`).catch(() => {});
        throw err;
      } finally {
        this.txDepth = Math.max(0, this.txDepth - 1);
      }
    };

    if (this.txDepth > 0) {
      return runTx();
    }
    return this.enqueue(runTx);
  }

  async transaction(workFn) {
    return this.executeTx(workFn);
  }
}

function attachWriteQueue(db) {
  if (!db) return db;
  if (db._rawRun && db.dbRun && db.getSetting && db.setSetting && db.nextNumber) {
    return db;
  }

  const origRun = db._rawRun || db.run.bind(db);
  const origGet = db._rawGet || db.get.bind(db);
  const origAll = db._rawAll || db.all.bind(db);
  const origExec = db._rawExec || db.exec.bind(db);
  const origPrepare = typeof db.prepare === 'function' ? db.prepare.bind(db) : null;
  const origSerialize = typeof db.serialize === 'function' ? db.serialize.bind(db) : null;

  db._rawRun = function rawRunWrapper(sql, params, callback) {
    if (typeof params === 'function') {
      callback = params;
      params = [];
    }
    return origRun(sql, params, function onRawComplete(err) {
      if (err && isBenignTxError(err)) {
        if (typeof callback === 'function') return callback.call(this, null);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof callback === 'function') {
        return callback.call(this, err);
      }
    });
  };

  db._rawGet = origGet;
  db._rawAll = origAll;
  db._rawExec = function rawExecWrapper(sql, callback) {
    return origExec(sql, function onExecComplete(err) {
      if (err && isBenignTxError(err)) {
        if (typeof callback === 'function') return callback.call(this, null);
        return;
      }
      if (typeof callback === 'function') {
        return callback.call(this, err);
      }
    });
  };
  if (origPrepare) db._rawPrepare = origPrepare;
  if (origSerialize) db._rawSerialize = origSerialize;

  db.run = db._rawRun;

  const queue = new DbWriteQueue(() => db);
  activeQueueInstance = queue;

  db._writeQueue = queue;
  db.writeQueue = queue;

  db.dbRun = queue.dbRun.bind(queue);
  db.dbGet = queue.dbGet.bind(queue);
  db.dbAll = queue.dbAll.bind(queue);
  db.dbExec = queue.dbExec.bind(queue);

  db.runAsync = queue.runAsync.bind(queue);
  db.getAsync = queue.getAsync.bind(queue);
  db.allAsync = queue.allAsync.bind(queue);
  db.execAsync = queue.execAsync.bind(queue);

  db.nextNumber = queue.nextNumber.bind(queue);
  db.getNextNumber = queue.nextNumber.bind(queue);

  db.executeTx = queue.executeTx.bind(queue);
  db.transaction = queue.transaction.bind(queue);
  db.withTransaction = queue.executeTx.bind(queue);
  db.runInTransaction = queue.executeTx.bind(queue);
  db.enqueueWrite = queue.enqueue.bind(queue);
  db.enqueue = queue.enqueue.bind(queue);

  if (typeof db.getSetting !== 'function') {
    db.getSetting = function getSetting(key, defaultValue = null, callback = null) {
      if (typeof defaultValue === 'function') {
        callback = defaultValue;
        defaultValue = null;
      }
      const p = queue
        .getAsync('SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?', [String(key)])
        .then((row) => (row && row.value !== undefined && row.value !== null ? row.value : defaultValue))
        .catch(() => defaultValue);

      if (typeof callback === 'function') {
        p.then((val) => callback(null, val));
      }
      return p;
    };
  }

  if (typeof db.setSetting !== 'function') {
    db.setSetting = function setSetting(key, value, callback = null) {
      const strKey = String(key);
      const strVal = value === undefined || value === null ? '' : String(value);
      const p = queue
        .runAsync(
          'INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value',
          [strKey, strVal]
        )
        .catch(() =>
          queue.runAsync('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)', [strKey, strVal])
        );

      if (typeof callback === 'function') {
        p.then((res) => callback(null, res)).catch((err) => callback(err));
      }
      return p;
    };
  }

  return db;
}

async function executeTx(workFn) {
  if (activeQueueInstance) {
    return activeQueueInstance.executeTx(workFn);
  }
  return workFn();
}

function getWriteQueue() {
  return activeQueueInstance;
}

module.exports = attachWriteQueue;
module.exports.attachWriteQueue = attachWriteQueue;
module.exports.DbWriteQueue = DbWriteQueue;
module.exports.executeTx = executeTx;
module.exports.getWriteQueue = getWriteQueue;

 * gherbi-enterprise-foundation.js
 * الترقية المعمارية النظيفة (100% Clean-Room Enterprise Modules) لنظام Gherbi.Ai POS
 * تضيف: التقسيط ومطابقة CCP، تقرير État 104، التكلفة المحملة (Landed Cost & WAC)،
 * تعدد المخازن والتحويلات، التوصيل والمندوبين (COD)، الكوبونات، الموارد البشرية (HR Payroll)،
 * تذاكر الصيانة (Atelier / SAV)، والرقابة الجنائية (Forensic Audit Log).
 */

'use strict';

const NEW_ENTERPRISE_SYNC_TABLES = [
  'warehouses',
  'stock_transfers',
  'stock_transfer_items',
  'installment_contracts',
  'installment_schedules',
  'ccp_reconciliation_logs',
  'drivers',
  'delivery_shipments',
  'discount_coupons',
  'employees',
  'payroll_payments',
  'service_tickets',
  'system_audit_logs'
];

function ensureSyncTriggerForTable(db, table) {
  db._rawRun(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_${table}_sync_insert`);
  db._rawRun(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS trg_${table}_sync_update`);

  db._rawRun(`
    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_${table}_sync_insert
    AFTER INSERT ON ${table}
    WHEN NEW.uuid IS NULL OR NEW.uuid = '' OR NEW.updated_at IS NULL OR NEW.updated_at = ''
    BEGIN
      UPDATE ${table}
      SET
        uuid = COALESCE(NULLIF(${table}.uuid, ''), lower(hex(randomblob(16)))),
        updated_at = COALESCE(NULLIF(${table}.updated_at, ''), strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now')),
        origin_device = COALESCE(NULLIF(${table}.origin_device, ''), (SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key='instance_id')),
        dirty = COALESCE(${table}.dirty, 1)
      WHERE rowid = NEW.rowid;
    END;
  `);

  db._rawRun(`
    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS trg_${table}_sync_update
    AFTER UPDATE ON ${table}
    WHEN NEW.updated_at IS OLD.updated_at AND NEW.server_rev IS OLD.server_rev
    BEGIN
      UPDATE ${table}
      SET
        updated_at = strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now'),
        origin_device = COALESCE((SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key='instance_id'), ${table}.origin_device),
        dirty = 1
      WHERE rowid = NEW.rowid;
    END;
  `);
}

    for (const tbl of NEW_ENTERPRISE_SYNC_TABLES) {

    db._rawRun('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inst_sched_contract ON installment_schedules(contract_id, status)');

    db._rawRun('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_coupons_code ON discount_coupons(code, is_active)');
    db._rawRun('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_action ON system_audit_logs(action_code, created_at)');
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // الدوال المحاسبية والتشغيلية للوحدات الـ 6 (Clean-Room Business Logic)
  // =========================================================================
  const api = {
    /**
     * 1. استخراج الكشف الضريبي السنوي الجزائري (État 104)
     */
    async getEtat104Report(year) {
      const targetYear = String(year || new Date().getFullYear());
      const dateFrom = `${targetYear}-01-01`;
      const dateTo = `${targetYear}-12-31`;

      return await db.dbAll(
        `
        SELECT
          c.id AS customer_id,
          c.name AS customer_name,
          COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(c.nif), ''), '---') AS nif,
          COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(c.rc), ''), '---') AS rc,
          COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(c.ai), ''), '---') AS ai,
          COALESCE(NULLIF(TRIM(c.nis), ''), '---') AS nis,
          COALESCE(c.address, '') AS address,
          COUNT(s.id) AS invoices_count,
          ROUND(SUM(
            CASE
              WHEN COALESCE(s.tax_percentage, 0) > 0
              THEN (s.total - COALESCE(s.stamp_amount, 0)) / (1.0 + (s.tax_percentage / 100.0))
              ELSE (s.total - COALESCE(s.stamp_amount, 0))
            END
          ), 2) AS total_ht,
          ROUND(SUM(
            CASE
              WHEN COALESCE(s.tax_percentage, 0) > 0
              THEN (s.total - COALESCE(s.stamp_amount, 0)) - ((s.total - COALESCE(s.stamp_amount, 0)) / (1.0 + (s.tax_percentage / 100.0)))
              ELSE 0
            END
          ), 2) AS total_tva,
          ROUND(SUM(COALESCE(s.stamp_amount, 0)), 2) AS total_stamp,
          ROUND(SUM(s.total), 2) AS total_ttc
        FROM customers c
        INNER JOIN sales s ON s.customer_id = c.id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL
        WHERE c.deleted_at IS NULL
          AND s.total > 0
          AND date(s.sale_date) BETWEEN date(?) AND date(?)
        GROUP BY c.id
        ORDER BY total_ttc DESC
        `,
        [dateFrom, dateTo]
      );
    },

    /**
     * 2. إنشاء عقد بيع بالتقسيط مع توليد جدول الأقساط الشهرية آلياً
     */
    async createInstallmentContract(payload = {}) {
      return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
        const customerId = Number(payload.customer_id || payload.customerId);
        if (!customerId) throw new Error('يرجى تحديد الزبون لإنشاء عقد التقسيط');

        const total = Number(payload.total_amount ?? payload.total ?? 0);
        const down = Number(payload.down_payment ?? payload.downPayment ?? 0);
        const financed = Math.max(0, Number((total - down).toFixed(2)));
        const months = Math.max(1, parseInt(payload.months_count ?? payload.monthsCount ?? 6, 10));
        const monthly = Number((financed / months).toFixed(2));
        const startDate = payload.start_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        const contractNo = payload.contract_number || `CNT-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;

        // تحديث بيانات CCP/RIP في بطاقة الزبون تلقائياً إن تم تزويدها
        if (payload.ccp_account || payload.bank_rip) {
          await tx.dbRun(
            `UPDATE customers
             SET ccp_account = COALESCE(NULLIF(?, ''), ccp_account),
                 ccp_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(?, ''), ccp_key),
                 bank_rip = COALESCE(NULLIF(?, ''), bank_rip)
             WHERE id = ?`,
            [payload.ccp_account || '', payload.ccp_key || '', payload.bank_rip || '', customerId]
          );
        }

        const ins = await tx.dbRun(
          `INSERT INTO installment_contracts (
            contract_number, customer_id, sale_id, total_amount, down_payment,
            financed_amount, months_count, monthly_amount, start_date,
            payment_method, ccp_account, ccp_key, bank_rip, guarantee_cheque_no, status, notes
          ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'active', ?)`,
          [
            contractNo,
            customerId,
            payload.sale_id || null,
            total,
            down,
            financed,
            months,
            monthly,
            startDate,
            payload.payment_method || 'CCP',
            payload.ccp_account || '',
            payload.ccp_key || '',
            payload.bank_rip || '',
            payload.guarantee_cheque_no || '',
            payload.notes || ''
          ]
        );

        const contractId = ins.lastID;
        const baseDate = new Date(startDate);
        let allocated = 0;

        for (let i = 1; i <= months; i++) {
          const due = new Date(baseDate);
          due.setMonth(due.getMonth() + i);
          const dueStr = due.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
          const instAmt = i === months ? Number((financed - allocated).toFixed(2)) : monthly;
          allocated = Number((allocated + instAmt).toFixed(2));

          await tx.dbRun(
            `INSERT INTO installment_schedules (
              plan_id, contract_id, customer_id, sale_id,
              installment_num, installment_no, due_date, amount, paid_amount, status
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 'pending')`,
            [contractId, contractId, customerId, payload.sale_id || null, i, i, dueStr, instAmt]
          );
        }

        return { id: contractId, contract_number: contractNo, monthly_amount: monthly, financed_amount: financed };
      });
    },

    /**
     * 3. جلب عقود التقسيط وجدول الأقساط
     */
    async getInstallmentContracts(filters = {}) {
      const conds = ['ic.deleted_at IS NULL'];
      const params = [];
      if (filters.customer_id) {
        conds.push('ic.customer_id = ?');
        params.push(Number(filters.customer_id));
      }
      if (filters.status && filters.status !== 'all') {
        conds.push('ic.status = ?');
        params.push(String(filters.status));
      }

      const contracts = await db.dbAll(
        `
        SELECT
          ic.*,
          c.name AS customer_name,
          c.phone AS customer_phone,
          COALESCE(NULLIF(ic.ccp_account, ''), c.ccp_account, '') AS effective_ccp,
          COALESCE(NULLIF(ic.ccp_key, ''), c.ccp_key, '') AS effective_ccp_key
        FROM installment_contracts ic
        LEFT JOIN customers c ON c.id = ic.customer_id
        WHERE ${conds.join(' AND ')}
        ORDER BY ic.id DESC
        `,
        params
      );

      const schedules = await db.dbAll(
        `
        SELECT
          s.*,
          COALESCE(s.contract_id, s.plan_id) AS resolved_contract_id,
          COALESCE(s.installment_no, s.installment_num, 1) AS resolved_no,
          c.name AS customer_name,
          c.phone AS customer_phone,
          ic.contract_number,
          COALESCE(NULLIF(ic.ccp_account, ''), c.ccp_account, '') AS ccp_account
        FROM installment_schedules s
        LEFT JOIN installment_contracts ic ON ic.id = COALESCE(s.contract_id, s.plan_id)
        LEFT JOIN customers c ON c.id = COALESCE(s.customer_id, ic.customer_id)
        WHERE s.deleted_at IS NULL
        ORDER BY date(s.due_date) ASC, s.id ASC
        `
      );

      return { contracts, schedules };
    },

    /**
     * 4. تسديد قسط شهري يدوياً أو جزئياً
     */
    async payInstallmentSchedule(payload = {}) {
      return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
        const scheduleId = Number(payload.schedule_id || payload.id);
        const payAmount = Number(payload.amount || 0);
        const payDate = payload.date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        if (!scheduleId || payAmount <= 0) throw new Error('مبلغ تسديد القسط غير صالح');

        const sched = await tx.dbGet(
          `SELECT s.*, COALESCE(s.contract_id, s.plan_id) AS cid, ic.customer_id AS ic_cust, ic.sale_id AS ic_sale
           FROM installment_schedules s
           LEFT JOIN installment_contracts ic ON ic.id = COALESCE(s.contract_id, s.plan_id)
           WHERE s.id = ? AND s.deleted_at IS NULL`,
          [scheduleId]
        );
        if (!sched) throw new Error('القسط المطلوب غير موجود');

        const custId = sched.customer_id || sched.ic_cust;
        const saleId = sched.sale_id || sched.ic_sale || null;
        const newPaid = Number((Number(sched.paid_amount || 0) + payAmount).toFixed(2));
        const newStatus = newPaid >= Number(sched.amount) - 0.01 ? 'paid' : 'partial';
        const receiptNo = await db.nextNumber('payment_receipt');

        const payIns = await tx.dbRun(
          `INSERT INTO sale_payments (sale_id, customer_id, amount, date, payment_method, reference, receipt_number, notes)
           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
          [
            saleId,
            custId,
            payAmount,
            payDate,
            payload.payment_method || 'ccp',
            payload.reference || '',
            receiptNo,
            payload.notes || `تسديد القسط رقم #${sched.installment_no || sched.installment_num || 1}`
          ]
        );

        if (saleId) {
          await tx.dbRun(
            'UPDATE sales SET paid_amount = MIN(total, COALESCE(paid_amount, 0) + ?) WHERE id = ?',
            [payAmount, saleId]
          );
        }

        await tx.dbRun(
          `UPDATE installment_schedules
           SET paid_amount = ?, status = ?, paid_at = ?, payment_id = ?
           WHERE id = ?`,
          [newPaid, newStatus, new Date().toISOString(), payIns.lastID, scheduleId]
        );

        // إذا اكتملت جميع أقساط العقد، نغير حالة العقد إلى completed
        if (sched.cid) {
          const openRow = await tx.dbGet(
            `SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM installment_schedules
             WHERE COALESCE(contract_id, plan_id) = ? AND status <> 'paid' AND deleted_at IS NULL`,
            [sched.cid]
          );
          if (openRow && Number(openRow.cnt) === 0) {
            await tx.dbRun("UPDATE installment_contracts SET status = 'completed' WHERE id = ?", [sched.cid]);
          }
        }

        return { success: true, payment_id: payIns.lastID, receipt_number: receiptNo, status: newStatus };
      });
    },

    /**
     * 5. محرك المطابقة الآلية لكشوفات الحساب البريدي (CCP Statement Reconciliation)
     */
    async reconcileCcpEntries(entries = [], fileName = 'ccp_statement.csv') {
      return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
        let matched = 0;
        let unmatched = 0;
        let duplicate = 0;

        for (const row of entries) {
          const acc = String(row.ccp_account || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
          const amt = Number(row.amount || 0);
          const ref = String(row.transaction_ref || '').trim();
          const dt = row.transaction_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

          if (!acc || amt <= 0) continue;

          if (ref) {
            const dup = await tx.dbGet(
              'SELECT id FROM ccp_reconciliation_logs WHERE transaction_ref = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL',
              [ref]
            );
            if (dup) {
              duplicate++;
              continue;
            }
          }

          const targetInst = await tx.dbGet(
            `
            SELECT s.id, COALESCE(s.contract_id, s.plan_id) AS contract_id, COALESCE(s.customer_id, c.customer_id) AS customer_id,
                   COALESCE(s.sale_id, c.sale_id) AS sale_id, s.amount, s.paid_amount
            FROM installment_schedules s
            LEFT JOIN installment_contracts c ON c.id = COALESCE(s.contract_id, s.plan_id) AND c.deleted_at IS NULL
            LEFT JOIN customers cu ON cu.id = COALESCE(s.customer_id, c.customer_id) AND cu.deleted_at IS NULL
            WHERE s.deleted_at IS NULL
              AND s.status IN ('pending', 'overdue', 'partial')
              AND (REPLACE(COALESCE(c.ccp_account, ''), ' ', '') = ? OR REPLACE(COALESCE(cu.ccp_account, ''), ' ', '') = ?)
            ORDER BY date(s.due_date) ASC, COALESCE(s.installment_no, s.installment_num) ASC
            LIMIT 1
            `,
            [acc, acc]
          );

          if (targetInst) {
            const newPaid = Number((Number(targetInst.paid_amount || 0) + amt).toFixed(2));
            const newStatus = newPaid >= Number(targetInst.amount) - 0.01 ? 'paid' : 'partial';
            const receiptNo = await db.nextNumber('payment_receipt');

            const payIns = await tx.dbRun(
              `INSERT INTO sale_payments (sale_id, customer_id, amount, date, payment_method, reference, receipt_number, notes)
               VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'ccp', ?, ?, ?)`,
              [
                targetInst.sale_id || null,
                targetInst.customer_id,
                amt,
                dt,
                ref,
                receiptNo,
                `تسديد قسط تلقائي عبر مطابقة CCP (${fileName})`
              ]
            );

            if (targetInst.sale_id) {
              await tx.dbRun(
                'UPDATE sales SET paid_amount = MIN(total, COALESCE(paid_amount, 0) + ?) WHERE id = ?',
                [amt, targetInst.sale_id]
              );
            }

            await tx.dbRun(
              `UPDATE installment_schedules
               SET paid_amount = ?, status = ?, paid_at = ?, payment_id = ?
               WHERE id = ?`,
              [newPaid, newStatus, new Date().toISOString(), payIns.lastID, targetInst.id]
            );

            await tx.dbRun(
              `INSERT INTO ccp_reconciliation_logs (
                batch_file_name, transaction_date, ccp_account, amount,
                transaction_ref, match_status, schedule_id, customer_id, details
              ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'MATCHED', ?, ?, 'تمت المطابقة وتسديد القسط آلياً')`,
              [fileName, dt, acc, amt, ref, targetInst.id, targetInst.customer_id]
            );
            matched++;
          } else {
            await tx.dbRun(
              `INSERT INTO ccp_reconciliation_logs (
                batch_file_name, transaction_date, ccp_account, amount,
                transaction_ref, match_status, details
              ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'UNMATCHED', 'لم يتم العثور على قسط مفتوح لهذا الحساب')`,
              [fileName, dt, acc, amt, ref]
            );
            unmatched++;
          }
        }

        return { matched, unmatched, duplicate };
      });
    },

    /**
     * 6. تحويل مخزني ذري بين مستودعين أو شاحنة توزيع (Stock Transfer)
     */
    async createStockTransfer(payload = {}) {
      return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
        const fromWh = Number(payload.from_warehouse_id || 1);
        const toWh = Number(payload.to_warehouse_id);
        const items = Array.isArray(payload.items) ? payload.items : [];
        if (!toWh || fromWh === toWh) throw new Error('يرجى اختيار مخزن وجهة مختلف عن المخزن المصدر');
        if (items.length === 0) throw new Error('قائمة المواد المحولة فارغة');

        const trfNo = `TRF-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;
        const trfDate = payload.transfer_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

        const ins = await tx.dbRun(
          `INSERT INTO stock_transfers (transfer_number, from_warehouse_id, to_warehouse_id, driver_id, status, notes, transfer_date, user_id)
           VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'completed', ?, ?, ?)`,
          [trfNo, fromWh, toWh, payload.driver_id || null, payload.notes || '', trfDate, payload.user_id || null]
        );
        const transferId = ins.lastID;

        for (const it of items) {
          const pid = Number(it.product_id);
          const qty = Number(it.quantity || 0);
          if (!pid || qty <= 0) continue;

          const srcBatches = await tx.dbAll(
            `SELECT * FROM inventory_batches
             WHERE product_id = ? AND COALESCE(warehouse_id, 1) = ? AND stock > 0 AND deleted_at IS NULL
             ORDER BY id ASC`,
            [pid, fromWh]
          );

          let rem = qty;
          let refBatch = srcBatches[0] || null;
          if (!refBatch) {
            refBatch = await tx.dbGet(
              'SELECT * FROM inventory_batches WHERE product_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1',
              [pid]
            );
          }

          for (const b of srcBatches) {
            if (rem <= 0) break;
            const take = Math.min(Number(b.stock || 0), rem);
            await tx.dbRun('UPDATE inventory_batches SET stock = stock - ? WHERE id = ?', [take, b.id]);
            rem = Number((rem - take).toFixed(4));
          }

          // إضافة الكمية في المخزن الوجهة
          const destBatch = await tx.dbGet(
            `SELECT id FROM inventory_batches
             WHERE product_id = ? AND COALESCE(warehouse_id, 1) = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL
             ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1`,
            [pid, toWh]
          );

          const costPrice = Number((refBatch && refBatch.cost_price) || it.cost_price || 0);
          const sellPrice = Number((refBatch && refBatch.selling_price) || it.selling_price || 0);
          const wholesalePrice = refBatch ? refBatch.wholesale_price : null;

          if (destBatch) {
            await tx.dbRun('UPDATE inventory_batches SET stock = COALESCE(stock, 0) + ? WHERE id = ?', [qty, destBatch.id]);
          } else {
            await tx.dbRun(
              `INSERT INTO inventory_batches (product_id, warehouse_id, stock, cost_price, selling_price, wholesale_price, location, shelf_etage, shelf_place)
               VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
              [pid, toWh, qty, costPrice, sellPrice, wholesalePrice, it.location || '', it.shelf_etage || '', it.shelf_place || '']
            );
          }

          await tx.dbRun(
            `INSERT INTO stock_transfer_items (transfer_id, product_id, batch_id, quantity, cost_price)
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
            [transferId, pid, refBatch ? refBatch.id : null, qty, costPrice]
          );
        }

        return { id: transferId, transfer_number: trfNo };
      });
    },

    /**
     * 7. تسجيل حدث في السجل الجنائي (Forensic Audit Log)
     */
    async logForensicAudit({ userId, userName, actionCode, entityTable, entityId, beforeObj, afterObj, details }) {
      return await db.dbRun(
        `INSERT INTO system_audit_logs (
          user_id, user_name, action_code, entity_table, entity_id, before_json, after_json, details
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
          userId || null,
          userName || 'System',
          actionCode,
          entityTable || '',
          entityId ? String(entityId) : '',
          beforeObj ? JSON.stringify(beforeObj) : null,
          afterObj ? JSON.stringify(afterObj) : null,
          details || ''
        ]
      );
    }
  };

  db.enterprise = api;
  return api;
}

module.exports = {
  NEW_ENTERPRISE_SYNC_TABLES,
  initEnterpriseFoundation
};



==================== FILE: gherbi-enterprise-ipc-and-lan.js ====================
/**
 * gherbi-enterprise-ipc-and-lan.js
 * المحرك الخلفي الموحد (IPC Handlers + Express REST Routes) للوحدات المؤسسية الـ 6 في Gherbi.Ai POS:
 * 1. عقود البيع بالتقسيط ومطابقة كشف CCP البريدي الآلية.
 * 2. الكشف الضريبي السنوي (État 104) وتوزيع التكلفة الاستيرادية (Landed Cost & WAC).
 * 3. المستودعات المتعددة، مواقع الرفوف، والتحويلات المخزنية الذرية.
 * 4. السائقون (Van Sales)، شحنات التوصيل، وتسوية التحصيل عند الاستلام (COD).
 * 5. كوبونات الخصم الترويجية والسجل الرقابي الجنائي (Forensic Audit Log).
 * 6. الموظفون ومسيّر الرواتب (HR Payroll) وتذاكر ورشة الصيانة (Atelier / SAV).
 */

'use strict';

const analyticsQueries = require('./analytics-queries');

function createEnterpriseOperations(db) {
  const ent = db.enterprise || {};

  // --- 1. التقسيط ومطابقة CCP ---
  async function getInstallmentContracts(filters = {}) {
    if (ent.getInstallmentContracts) return await ent.getInstallmentContracts(filters);
    return { contracts: [], schedules: [] };
  }

  async function createInstallmentContract(payload = {}) {
    if (ent.createInstallmentContract) return await ent.createInstallmentContract(payload);
    throw new Error('Enterprise foundation not initialized');
  }

  async function payInstallmentSchedule(payload = {}) {
    if (ent.payInstallmentSchedule) return await ent.payInstallmentSchedule(payload);
    throw new Error('Enterprise foundation not initialized');
  }

  async function reconcileCcpStatement(payload = {}) {
    const entries = Array.isArray(payload.entries) ? payload.entries : [];
    const fileName = payload.fileName || payload.batch_file_name || 'ccp_statement.csv';
    if (ent.reconcileCcpEntries) return await ent.reconcileCcpEntries(entries, fileName);
    return { matched: 0, unmatched: 0, duplicate: 0 };
  }

  async function getCcpReconciliationLogs(filters = {}) {
    const limit = Math.max(10, Number(filters.limit || 200));
    return await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT l.*, c.name AS customer_name
       FROM ccp_reconciliation_logs l
       LEFT JOIN customers c ON c.id = l.customer_id
       WHERE l.deleted_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY l.id DESC LIMIT ?`,
      [limit]
    );
  }

  // --- 2. الكشف الضريبي السنوي État 104 + تحديث Landed Cost & WAC ---
  async function getEtat104(params = {}) {
    const year = typeof params === 'object' ? params.year : params;
    return await analyticsQueries.getEtat104Report(db, { year });
  }

  async function applyLandedCostAndWacToPurchase(invoiceId, data = {}) {
    const invId = Number(invoiceId);
    if (!invId) return;
    const freight = Number(data.freight_cost || 0);
    const customs = Number(data.customs_cost || 0);
    const handling = Number(data.handling_cost || 0);
    const totalLandedExtra = Number((freight + customs + handling).toFixed(2));

    await db.dbRun(
      `UPDATE purchase_invoices
       SET freight_cost = ?, customs_cost = ?, handling_cost = ?,
           additional_costs = COALESCE(additional_costs, 0) + ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [freight, customs, handling, totalLandedExtra > 0 && !data.additional_costs ? totalLandedExtra : 0, invId]
    );

    const pItems = await db.dbAll(
      'SELECT * FROM purchase_items WHERE invoice_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL',
      [invId]
    );

    const totalQty = pItems.reduce((s, it) => s + Math.max(0, Number(it.quantity || 0)), 0);
    const landedPerUnit = totalQty > 0 && totalLandedExtra > 0 && !data.additional_costs
      ? totalLandedExtra / totalQty
      : 0;

    for (const it of pItems) {
      if (!it.product_id) continue;
      if (landedPerUnit > 0 && it.batch_id) {
        await db.dbRun(
          'UPDATE inventory_batches SET cost_price = ROUND(cost_price + ?, 2) WHERE id = ?',
          [landedPerUnit, it.batch_id]
        );
      }

      // حساب وتحديث التكلفة المتوسطة المرجحة (WAC) لجميع دفعات المنتج النشطة
      const wacRow = await db.dbGet(
        `SELECT
           SUM(CASE WHEN stock > 0 THEN stock * cost_price ELSE 0 END) AS total_val,
           SUM(CASE WHEN stock > 0 THEN stock ELSE 0 END) AS total_stk
         FROM inventory_batches
         WHERE product_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL`,
        [it.product_id]
      );
      const wac = wacRow && Number(wacRow.total_stk) > 0
        ? Number((Number(wacRow.total_val) / Number(wacRow.total_stk)).toFixed(2))
        : Number(it.cost_price || 0);

      await db.dbRun(
        'UPDATE inventory_batches SET wac_cost = ? WHERE product_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL',
        [wac, it.product_id]
      );
    }
  }

  // --- 3. المستودعات، الرفوف، والتحويلات المخزنية ---
  async function getWarehouses() {
    const list = await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT w.*, d.name AS driver_name,
              (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(ib.stock), 0) FROM inventory_batches ib WHERE COALESCE(ib.warehouse_id, 1) = w.id AND ib.deleted_at IS NULL) AS total_stock_units,
              (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN ib.stock > 0 THEN ib.stock * ib.cost_price ELSE 0 END), 0) FROM inventory_batches ib WHERE COALESCE(ib.warehouse_id, 1) = w.id AND ib.deleted_at IS NULL) AS total_stock_value
       FROM warehouses w
       LEFT JOIN drivers d ON d.id = w.driver_id
       WHERE w.deleted_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY w.is_default DESC, w.id ASC`
    );
    return list;
  }

  async function saveWarehouse(payload = {}) {
    const id = payload.id ? Number(payload.id) : null;
    const name = String(payload.name || '').trim();
    if (!name) throw new Error('يرجى إدخال اسم المستودع / الشاحنة');

    if (id) {
      await db.dbRun(
        'UPDATE warehouses SET name = ?, location = ?, type = ?, driver_id = ? WHERE id = ?',
        [name, payload.location || '', payload.type || 'main', payload.driver_id || null, id]
      );
      return { id, success: true };
    }
    const ins = await db.dbRun(
      'INSERT INTO warehouses (name, location, type, driver_id, is_default) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0)',
      [name, payload.location || '', payload.type || 'branch', payload.driver_id || null]
    );
    return { id: ins.lastID, success: true };
  }

  async function deleteWarehouse(id) {
    const wid = Number(id);
    if (wid === 1) throw new Error('لا يمكن حذف المخزن الرئيسي الافتراضي');
    await db.dbRun('DELETE FROM warehouses WHERE id = ?', [wid]);
    return { success: true };
  }

  async function getStockTransfers() {
    const transfers = await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT st.*, w1.name AS from_warehouse_name, w2.name AS to_warehouse_name, d.name AS driver_name,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stock_transfer_items sti WHERE sti.transfer_id = st.id AND sti.deleted_at IS NULL) AS items_count,
              (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(sti.quantity), 0) FROM stock_transfer_items sti WHERE sti.transfer_id = st.id AND sti.deleted_at IS NULL) AS total_qty
       FROM stock_transfers st
       LEFT JOIN warehouses w1 ON w1.id = st.from_warehouse_id
       LEFT JOIN warehouses w2 ON w2.id = st.to_warehouse_id
       LEFT JOIN drivers d ON d.id = st.driver_id
       WHERE st.deleted_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY st.id DESC LIMIT 200`
    );
    return transfers;
  }

  async function createStockTransfer(payload = {}) {
    if (ent.createStockTransfer) return await ent.createStockTransfer(payload);
    throw new Error('Transfer engine not ready');
  }

  async function updateBatchShelfLocation(payload = {}) {
    const productId = Number(payload.product_id || payload.productId);
    const batchId = payload.batch_id ? Number(payload.batch_id) : null;
    const whId = Number(payload.warehouse_id || 1);
    const etage = String(payload.shelf_etage || '').trim();
    const place = String(payload.shelf_place || '').trim();
    const loc = [etage ? 'طابق: ' + etage : '', place ? 'رف: ' + place : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' | ');

    if (batchId) {
      await db.dbRun(
        'UPDATE inventory_batches SET warehouse_id = ?, shelf_etage = ?, shelf_place = ?, location = COALESCE(NULLIF(?, ""), location) WHERE id = ?',
        [whId, etage, place, loc, batchId]
      );
    } else if (productId) {
      await db.dbRun(
        'UPDATE inventory_batches SET warehouse_id = ?, shelf_etage = ?, shelf_place = ?, location = COALESCE(NULLIF(?, ""), location) WHERE product_id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL',
        [whId, etage, place, loc, productId]
      );
    }
    return { success: true };
  }

  // --- 4. السائقون (Van Sales) وشحنات التوصيل وتحصيل COD ---
  async function getDrivers() {
    return await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT d.*, w.name AS warehouse_name
       FROM drivers d
       LEFT JOIN warehouses w ON w.id = d.warehouse_id
       WHERE d.deleted_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY d.id DESC`
    );
  }

  async function saveDriver(payload = {}) {
    const id = payload.id ? Number(payload.id) : null;
    const name = String(payload.name || '').trim();
    if (!name) throw new Error('يرجى إدخال اسم السائق / الموزع');

    if (id) {
      await db.dbRun(
        `UPDATE drivers
         SET name = ?, phone = ?, vehicle_name = ?, vehicle_plate = ?,
             warehouse_id = ?, commission_rate = ?, is_active = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [
          name,
          payload.phone || '',
          payload.vehicle_name || '',
          payload.vehicle_plate || '',
          payload.warehouse_id || null,
          Number(payload.commission_rate || 0),
          payload.is_active !== false ? 1 : 0,
          id
        ]
      );
      return { id, success: true };
    }
    const ins = await db.dbRun(
      `INSERT INTO drivers (name, phone, vehicle_name, vehicle_plate, warehouse_id, commission_rate, is_active)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1)`,
      [
        name,
        payload.phone || '',
        payload.vehicle_name || '',
        payload.vehicle_plate || '',
        payload.warehouse_id || null,
        Number(payload.commission_rate || 0)
      ]
    );
    return { id: ins.lastID, success: true };
  }

  async function deleteDriver(id) {
    await db.dbRun('DELETE FROM drivers WHERE id = ?', [Number(id)]);
    return { success: true };
  }

  async function getDeliveryShipments(filters = {}) {
    const conds = ['ds.deleted_at IS NULL'];
    const params = [];
    if (filters.status && filters.status !== 'all') {
      conds.push('ds.status = ?');
      params.push(String(filters.status));
    }
    if (filters.driver_id) {
      conds.push('ds.driver_id = ?');
      params.push(Number(filters.driver_id));
    }
    return await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT ds.*, d.name AS driver_name, d.phone AS driver_phone, d.vehicle_plate
       FROM delivery_shipments ds
       LEFT JOIN drivers d ON d.id = ds.driver_id
       WHERE ${conds.join(' AND ')}
       ORDER BY ds.id DESC LIMIT 300`,
      params
    );
  }

  async function saveDeliveryShipment(payload = {}) {
    const id = payload.id ? Number(payload.id) : null;
    const docNo = payload.doc_number || (await db.nextNumber('delivery'));

    if (id) {
      await db.dbRun(
        `UPDATE delivery_shipments
         SET customer_name = ?, customer_phone = ?, customer_address = ?, wilaya = ?, commune = ?,
             driver_id = ?, carrier_name = ?, tracking_code = ?, shipping_fee = ?, cod_amount = ?,
             status = ?, delivery_date = ?, notes = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [
          payload.customer_name || 'زبون',
          payload.customer_phone || '',
          payload.customer_address || '',
          payload.wilaya || '',
          payload.commune || '',
          payload.driver_id || null,
          payload.carrier_name || '',
          payload.tracking_code || '',
          Number(payload.shipping_fee || 0),
          Number(payload.cod_amount || 0),
          payload.status || 'preparing',
          payload.delivery_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
          payload.notes || '',
          id
        ]
      );
      return { id, doc_number: docNo, success: true };
    }

    const ins = await db.dbRun(
      `INSERT INTO delivery_shipments (
        doc_number, sale_id, order_id, customer_id, customer_name, customer_phone, customer_address,
        wilaya, commune, driver_id, carrier_name, tracking_code, shipping_fee, cod_amount,
        collected_amount, status, delivery_date, notes
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        docNo,
        payload.sale_id || null,
        payload.order_id || null,
        payload.customer_id || null,
        payload.customer_name || 'زبون',
        payload.customer_phone || '',
        payload.customer_address || '',
        payload.wilaya || '',
        payload.commune || '',
        payload.driver_id || null,
        payload.carrier_name || '',
        payload.tracking_code || '',
        Number(payload.shipping_fee || 0),
        Number(payload.cod_amount || 0),
        payload.status || 'preparing',
        payload.delivery_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
        payload.notes || ''
      ]
    );
    return { id: ins.lastID, doc_number: docNo, success: true };
  }

  async function settleShipmentCod(payload = {}) {
    return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
      const shipmentId = Number(payload.shipment_id || payload.id);
      const status = payload.status || 'delivered';
      const sh = await tx.dbGet('SELECT * FROM delivery_shipments WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL', [shipmentId]);
      if (!sh) throw new Error('شحنة التوصيل غير موجودة');

      const collected = payload.collected_amount !== undefined ? Number(payload.collected_amount) : Number(sh.cod_amount || 0);
      await tx.dbRun(
        'UPDATE delivery_shipments SET status = ?, collected_amount = ?, delivery_date = ? WHERE id = ?',
        [status, status === 'delivered' ? collected : 0, new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), shipmentId]
      );

      if (status === 'delivered' && collected > 0) {
        if (sh.sale_id) {
          await tx.dbRun(
            'UPDATE sales SET paid_amount = MIN(total, COALESCE(paid_amount, 0) + ?) WHERE id = ?',
            [collected, sh.sale_id]
          );
        }
        if (sh.order_id) {
          await tx.dbRun("UPDATE customer_orders SET status = 'delivered' WHERE id = ?", [sh.order_id]);
        }

        const openSess = await tx.dbGet(
          "SELECT id FROM caisse_sessions WHERE status = 'open' AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        );
        if (openSess && openSess.id) {
          await tx.dbRun(
            `INSERT INTO caisse_transactions (session_id, type, amount, category, description, reference_id)
             VALUES (?, 'cash_in', ?, 'تحصيل توصيل COD', ?, ?)`,
            [openSess.id, collected, `تحصيل شحنة توصيل (${sh.doc_number || '#' + sh.id}) — ${sh.customer_name}`, sh.id]
          );
        }
      }

      return { success: true, status, collected_amount: collected };
    });
  }

  // --- 5. كوبونات الخصم والسجل الرقابي الجنائي ---
  async function getDiscountCoupons() {
    return await db.dbAll('SELECT * FROM discount_coupons WHERE deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC');
  }

  async function saveDiscountCoupon(payload = {}) {
    const id = payload.id ? Number(payload.id) : null;
    const code = String(payload.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!code) throw new Error('يرجى إدخال كود الكوبون');

    if (id) {
      await db.dbRun(
        `UPDATE discount_coupons
         SET code = ?, discount_type = ?, discount_value = ?, min_invoice_total = ?, max_uses = ?, expiry_date = ?, is_active = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [
          code,
          payload.discount_type || 'percent',
          Number(payload.discount_value || 0),
          Number(payload.min_invoice_total || 0),
          Number(payload.max_uses || 100),
          payload.expiry_date || null,
          payload.is_active !== false ? 1 : 0,
          id
        ]
      );
      return { id, code, success: true };
    }

    const ins = await db.dbRun(
      `INSERT INTO discount_coupons (code, discount_type, discount_value, min_invoice_total, max_uses, used_count, expiry_date, is_active)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, 1)`,
      [
        code,
        payload.discount_type || 'percent',
        Number(payload.discount_value || 0),
        Number(payload.min_invoice_total || 0),
        Number(payload.max_uses || 100),
        payload.expiry_date || null
      ]
    );
    return { id: ins.lastID, code, success: true };
  }

  async function deleteDiscountCoupon(id) {
    await db.dbRun('DELETE FROM discount_coupons WHERE id = ?', [Number(id)]);
    return { success: true };
  }

  async function validateDiscountCoupon(payload = {}) {
    const code = String(payload.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const subtotal = Number(payload.subtotal || payload.invoiceTotal || 0);
    if (!code) throw new Error('أدخل كود الكوبون أولاً');

    const cp = await db.dbGet(
      'SELECT * FROM discount_coupons WHERE UPPER(code) = ? AND is_active = 1 AND deleted_at IS NULL LIMIT 1',
      [code]
    );
    if (!cp) throw new Error('كود الكوبون غير صحيح أو غير مفعل');

    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (cp.expiry_date && String(cp.expiry_date).slice(0, 10) < today) {
      throw new Error('انتهت صلاحية هذا الكوبون بتاريخ ' + cp.expiry_date);
    }
    if (cp.max_uses && Number(cp.used_count || 0) >= Number(cp.max_uses)) {
      throw new Error('تم استنفاد الحد الأقصى لاستخدامات هذا الكوبون');
    }
    if (cp.min_invoice_total && subtotal < Number(cp.min_invoice_total)) {
      throw new Error(`الحد الأدنى للفاتورة لتطبيق هذا الكوبون هو ${Number(cp.min_invoice_total).toFixed(2)} د.ج`);
    }

    const discountAmount = cp.discount_type === 'percent'
      ? Number(((subtotal * Number(cp.discount_value || 0)) / 100).toFixed(2))
      : Math.min(subtotal, Number(cp.discount_value || 0));

    return {
      valid: true,
      coupon: cp,
      discount_amount: discountAmount
    };
  }

  async function consumeDiscountCoupon(payload = {}) {
    const code = String(payload.code || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const saleId = payload.sale_id ? Number(payload.sale_id) : null;
    const discAmt = Number(payload.discount_amount || 0);
    if (!code) return { ok: false };
    await db.dbRun(
      'UPDATE discount_coupons SET used_count = COALESCE(used_count, 0) + 1 WHERE UPPER(code) = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL',
      [code]
    );
    if (saleId) {
      await db.dbRun(
        'UPDATE sales SET coupon_code = ?, coupon_discount = ? WHERE id = ?',
        [code, discAmt, saleId]
      );
    }
    return { ok: true, code };
  }

  async function getSystemAuditLogs(filters = {}) {
    const limit = Math.max(20, Number(filters.limit || 250));
    return await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT * FROM system_audit_logs
       WHERE deleted_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?`,
      [limit]
    );
  }

  async function logSystemAudit(payload = {}) {
    if (ent.logForensicAudit) return await ent.logForensicAudit(payload);
    return { ok: true };
  }

  // --- 6. الموظفون ومسيّر الرواتب (HR Payroll) وتذاكر الصيانة (Atelier / SAV) ---
  async function getEmployees() {
    return await db.dbAll('SELECT * FROM employees WHERE deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC');
  }

  async function saveEmployee(payload = {}) {
    const id = payload.id ? Number(payload.id) : null;
    const name = String(payload.name || '').trim();
    if (!name) throw new Error('يرجى إدخال اسم الموظف');

    if (id) {
      await db.dbRun(
        `UPDATE employees
         SET name = ?, role_title = ?, phone = ?, base_salary = ?, commission_percent = ?, hire_date = ?, is_active = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [
          name,
          payload.role_title || 'بائع',
          payload.phone || '',
          Number(payload.base_salary || 0),
          Number(payload.commission_percent || 0),
          payload.hire_date || null,
          payload.is_active !== false ? 1 : 0,
          id
        ]
      );
      return { id, success: true };
    }
    const ins = await db.dbRun(
      `INSERT INTO employees (name, role_title, phone, base_salary, commission_percent, hire_date, is_active)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 1)`,
      [
        name,
        payload.role_title || 'بائع',
        payload.phone || '',
        Number(payload.base_salary || 0),
        Number(payload.commission_percent || 0),
        payload.hire_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
      ]
    );
    return { id: ins.lastID, success: true };
  }

  async function deleteEmployee(id) {
    await db.dbRun('DELETE FROM employees WHERE id = ?', [Number(id)]);
    return { success: true };
  }

  async function getPayrollPayments() {
    return await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT pp.*, e.name AS employee_name, e.role_title
       FROM payroll_payments pp
       LEFT JOIN employees e ON e.id = pp.employee_id
       WHERE pp.deleted_at IS NULL
       ORDER BY pp.id DESC LIMIT 250`
    );
  }

  async function payEmployeeSalary(payload = {}) {
    return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
      const empId = Number(payload.employee_id);
      const emp = await tx.dbGet('SELECT * FROM employees WHERE id = ?', [empId]);
      if (!emp) throw new Error('الموظف غير موجود');

      const baseAmt = Number(payload.base_amount ?? emp.base_salary ?? 0);
      const bonusAmt = Number(payload.bonus_amount || 0);
      const deductAmt = Number(payload.deduction_amount || 0);
      const netPaid = Math.max(0, Number((baseAmt + bonusAmt - deductAmt).toFixed(2)));
      const payDate = payload.payment_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const periodMonth = payload.period_month || payDate.slice(0, 7);

      const openSess = await tx.dbGet(
        "SELECT id FROM caisse_sessions WHERE status = 'open' AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
      );
      const sessId = openSess ? openSess.id : null;

      // 1) تسجيل الراتب تلقائياً في جدول المصاريف (expenses)
      const expDesc = `صرف راتب الموظف: ${emp.name} — شهر (${periodMonth})`;
      const expIns = await tx.dbRun(
        'INSERT INTO expenses (category, description, amount, date, session_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ['رواتب', expDesc, netPaid, payDate, sessId]
      );

      // 2) تسجيل حركة سحب في الصندوق المفتوح (caisse_transactions)
      if (sessId) {
        await tx.dbRun(
          `INSERT INTO caisse_transactions (session_id, type, amount, category, description, reference_id)
           VALUES (?, 'expense', ?, 'رواتب', ?, ?)`,
          [sessId, netPaid, expDesc, expIns.lastID]
        );
      }

      // 3) حفظ مسير الراتب في payroll_payments
      const payIns = await tx.dbRun(
        `INSERT INTO payroll_payments (
          employee_id, period_month, base_amount, bonus_amount, deduction_amount,
          net_paid, payment_date, expense_id, session_id, notes
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [empId, periodMonth, baseAmt, bonusAmt, deductAmt, netPaid, payDate, expIns.lastID, sessId, payload.notes || '']
      );

      return { id: payIns.lastID, expense_id: expIns.lastID, net_paid: netPaid, success: true };
    });
  }

  async function getServiceTickets(filters = {}) {
    const conds = ['st.deleted_at IS NULL'];
    const params = [];
    if (filters.status && filters.status !== 'all') {
      conds.push('st.status = ?');
      params.push(String(filters.status));
    }
    return await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT st.* FROM service_tickets st
       WHERE ${conds.join(' AND ')}
       ORDER BY st.id DESC LIMIT 250`,
      params
    );
  }

  async function saveServiceTicket(payload = {}) {
    const id = payload.id ? Number(payload.id) : null;
    const ticketNo = payload.ticket_number || `SAV-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;

    if (id) {
      await db.dbRun(
        `UPDATE service_tickets
         SET customer_id = ?, customer_name = ?, customer_phone = ?, item_model = ?, serial_or_plate = ?,
             problem_desc = ?, diagnosis_notes = ?, estimated_cost = ?, deposit_paid = ?, final_total = ?,
             status = ?, technician_name = ?, promised_date = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [
          payload.customer_id || null,
          payload.customer_name || 'زبون صيانة',
          payload.customer_phone || '',
          payload.item_model || '',
          payload.serial_or_plate || '',
          payload.problem_desc || '',
          payload.diagnosis_notes || '',
          Number(payload.estimated_cost || 0),
          Number(payload.deposit_paid || 0),
          Number(payload.final_total ?? payload.estimated_cost ?? 0),
          payload.status || 'received',
          payload.technician_name || '',
          payload.promised_date || null,
          id
        ]
      );
      return { id, ticket_number: ticketNo, success: true };
    }

    const ins = await db.dbRun(
      `INSERT INTO service_tickets (
        ticket_number, customer_id, customer_name, customer_phone, item_model, serial_or_plate,
        problem_desc, diagnosis_notes, estimated_cost, deposit_paid, final_total,
        status, technician_name, promised_date
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        ticketNo,
        payload.customer_id || null,
        payload.customer_name || 'زبون صيانة',
        payload.customer_phone || '',
        payload.item_model || 'جهاز صيانة',
        payload.serial_or_plate || '',
        payload.problem_desc || '',
        payload.diagnosis_notes || '',
        Number(payload.estimated_cost || 0),
        Number(payload.deposit_paid || 0),
        Number(payload.final_total ?? payload.estimated_cost ?? 0),
        payload.status || 'received',
        payload.technician_name || '',
        payload.promised_date || null
      ]
    );
    return { id: ins.lastID, ticket_number: ticketNo, success: true };
  }

  async function convertServiceTicketToSale(payload = {}) {
    return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
      const ticketId = Number(payload.ticket_id || payload.id);
      const tk = await tx.dbGet('SELECT * FROM service_tickets WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL', [ticketId]);
      if (!tk) throw new Error('تذكرة الصيانة غير موجودة');

      const totalAmt = Number(payload.final_total ?? tk.final_total ?? tk.estimated_cost ?? 0);
      const paidAmt = Number(payload.paid_amount ?? totalAmt);
      const docNo = await db.nextNumber('invoice');
      const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

      const openSess = await tx.dbGet(
        "SELECT id FROM caisse_sessions WHERE status = 'open' AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
      );

      const saleIns = await tx.dbRun(
        `INSERT INTO sales (doc_number, customer_id, subtotal, total, paid_amount, sale_date, session_id, payment_method)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'cash')`,
        [docNo, tk.customer_id || null, totalAmt, totalAmt, paidAmt, nowIso, openSess ? openSess.id : null]
      );
      const saleId = saleIns.lastID;

      await tx.dbRun(
        `INSERT INTO sale_items (sale_id, quantity, price, subtotal, custom_product_name, serial_number)
         VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [saleId, totalAmt, totalAmt, `خدمة صيانة وإصلاح (${tk.ticket_number}): ${tk.item_model}`, tk.serial_or_plate || '']
      );

      if (paidAmt > 0) {
        await tx.dbRun(
          'INSERT INTO sale_payments (sale_id, customer_id, amount, date, payment_method) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, "cash")',
          [saleId, tk.customer_id || null, paidAmt, nowIso.slice(0, 10)]
        );
      }

      await tx.dbRun(
        "UPDATE service_tickets SET status = 'delivered', final_total = ?, sale_id = ? WHERE id = ?",
        [totalAmt, saleId, ticketId]
      );

      return { success: true, sale_id: saleId, doc_number: docNo };
    });
  }


  // --- 7. عمليات الجملة والقطاعات: آخر أسعار الزبون، الصناديق المسترجعة (Consignes)، وورقة شحن الشاحنة ---
  async function getCustomerLastPrices(payload = {}) {
    const customerId = Number(typeof payload === 'object' ? (payload.customer_id || payload.customerId) : payload);
    if (!customerId) return { prices: {}, customer: null };

    const customer = await db.dbGet(
      `SELECT c.*,
              (COALESCE((SELECT SUM(s.total - s.paid_amount) FROM sales s WHERE s.customer_id = c.id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL), 0) +
               (COALESCE(c.initial_debt, 0) - COALESCE(c.initial_debt_paid, 0))) AS current_debt
       FROM customers c
       WHERE c.id = ? AND c.deleted_at IS NULL`,
      [customerId]
    );

    const rows = await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT si.product_id, si.price, si.quantity, COALESCE(si.bonus_qty, 0) AS bonus_qty,
              s.sale_date, s.doc_number
       FROM sale_items si
       INNER JOIN sales s ON s.id = si.sale_id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL
       WHERE s.customer_id = ? AND si.product_id IS NOT NULL AND si.deleted_at IS NULL AND si.price > 0
       ORDER BY s.id DESC
       LIMIT 600`,
      [customerId]
    );

    const prices = {};
    for (const r of rows) {
      const pid = Number(r.product_id);
      if (!prices[pid]) {
        prices[pid] = {
          price: Number(r.price || 0),
          last_qty: Number(r.quantity || 1),
          last_bonus: Number(r.bonus_qty || 0),
          sale_date: r.sale_date,
          doc_number: r.doc_number
        };
      }
    }

    return { customer: customer || null, prices };
  }

  async function getReturnableContainers(filters = {}) {
    const conds = ['rc.deleted_at IS NULL'];
    const params = [];
    if (filters.customer_id || filters.customerId) {
      conds.push('rc.customer_id = ?');
      params.push(Number(filters.customer_id || filters.customerId));
    }
    if (filters.status && filters.status !== 'all') {
      conds.push('rc.status = ?');
      params.push(String(filters.status));
    }
    return await db.dbAll(
      `SELECT rc.*, COALESCE(c.name, rc.customer_name, 'زبون') AS resolved_customer_name, c.phone AS customer_phone
       FROM returnable_containers rc
       LEFT JOIN customers c ON c.id = rc.customer_id
       WHERE ${conds.join(' AND ')}
       ORDER BY rc.id DESC LIMIT 300`,
      params
    );
  }

  async function saveReturnableContainer(payload = {}) {
    return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
      const id = payload.id ? Number(payload.id) : null;
      const customerId = payload.customer_id ? Number(payload.customer_id) : null;
      let customerName = String(payload.customer_name || '').trim();
      if (customerId && !customerName) {
        const cRow = await tx.dbGet('SELECT name FROM customers WHERE id = ?', [customerId]);
        if (cRow) customerName = cRow.name;
      }
      const containerName = String(payload.container_name || 'صندوق بلاستيكي (Caisse)').trim();
      const qtyOut = Math.max(0, Number(payload.qty_out || 0));
      const qtyReturned = Math.max(0, Number(payload.qty_returned || 0));
      const depositAmt = Math.max(0, Number(payload.deposit_amount || 0));
      const outDate = payload.out_date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const docNo = payload.doc_number || `CSG-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`;
      const status = qtyReturned >= qtyOut && qtyOut > 0 ? 'returned' : qtyReturned > 0 ? 'partial' : 'out';

      if (id) {
        await tx.dbRun(
          `UPDATE returnable_containers
           SET customer_id = ?, customer_name = ?, container_name = ?, qty_out = ?, qty_returned = ?,
               deposit_amount = ?, status = ?, notes = ?
           WHERE id = ?`,
          [customerId, customerName || 'زبون', containerName, qtyOut, qtyReturned, depositAmt, status, payload.notes || '', id]
        );
        return { id, doc_number: docNo, success: true };
      }

      const ins = await tx.dbRun(
        `INSERT INTO returnable_containers (
          doc_number, customer_id, customer_name, container_name, qty_out, qty_returned,
          deposit_amount, refunded_amount, status, out_date, notes
        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [docNo, customerId, customerName || 'زبون', containerName, qtyOut, qtyReturned, depositAmt, status, outDate, payload.notes || '']
      );

      if (depositAmt > 0 && payload.record_in_caisse !== false) {
        const openSess = await tx.dbGet(
          "SELECT id FROM caisse_sessions WHERE status = 'open' AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        );
        if (openSess && openSess.id) {
          await tx.dbRun(
            `INSERT INTO caisse_transactions (session_id, type, amount, category, description, reference_id)
             VALUES (?, 'cash_in', ?, 'أمانات صناديق Consignes', ?, ?)`,
            [openSess.id, depositAmt, `تأمين صناديق/طبلات (${docNo}) — ${customerName || 'زبون'} (${qtyOut} ${containerName})`, ins.lastID]
          );
        }
      }

      return { id: ins.lastID, doc_number: docNo, success: true };
    });
  }

  async function settleReturnableContainer(payload = {}) {
    return await db.withTransaction(async (tx) => {
      const id = Number(payload.id || payload.container_id);
      const rec = await tx.dbGet('SELECT * FROM returnable_containers WHERE id = ? AND deleted_at IS NULL', [id]);
      if (!rec) throw new Error('سجل الأمانات غير موجود');

      const addReturnQty = payload.add_returned_qty !== undefined
        ? Number(payload.add_returned_qty)
        : Math.max(0, Number(rec.qty_out || 0) - Number(rec.qty_returned || 0));
      const newReturned = Math.min(Number(rec.qty_out || 0), Number(rec.qty_returned || 0) + addReturnQty);
      const refundAmt = payload.refund_amount !== undefined
        ? Number(payload.refund_amount)
        : (newReturned >= Number(rec.qty_out || 0) ? Math.max(0, Number(rec.deposit_amount || 0) - Number(rec.refunded_amount || 0)) : 0);

      const newRefunded = Number(rec.refunded_amount || 0) + refundAmt;
      const newStatus = newReturned >= Number(rec.qty_out || 0) ? 'returned' : newReturned > 0 ? 'partial' : 'out';
      const retDate = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

      await tx.dbRun(
        `UPDATE returnable_containers
         SET qty_returned = ?, refunded_amount = ?, status = ?, return_date = ?
         WHERE id = ?`,
        [newReturned, newRefunded, newStatus, retDate, id]
      );

      if (refundAmt > 0 && payload.record_in_caisse !== false) {
        const openSess = await tx.dbGet(
          "SELECT id FROM caisse_sessions WHERE status = 'open' AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        );
        if (openSess && openSess.id) {
          await tx.dbRun(
            `INSERT INTO caisse_transactions (session_id, type, amount, category, description, reference_id)
             VALUES (?, 'cash_out', ?, 'إرجاع أمانات صناديق', ?, ?)`,
            [openSess.id, refundAmt, `استرجاع أمانة صناديق (${rec.doc_number || '#' + rec.id}) — ${rec.customer_name}`, rec.id]
          );
        }
      }

      return { success: true, id, qty_returned: newReturned, status: newStatus, refunded_amount: newRefunded };
    });
  }

  async function generateTruckLoadingSheet(payload = {}) {
    const saleIds = Array.isArray(payload.sale_ids) ? payload.sale_ids.map(Number).filter(Boolean) : [];
    const orderIds = Array.isArray(payload.order_ids) ? payload.order_ids.map(Number).filter(Boolean) : [];
    const shipmentIds = Array.isArray(payload.shipment_ids) ? payload.shipment_ids.map(Number).filter(Boolean) : [];
    const driverId = payload.driver_id ? Number(payload.driver_id) : null;
    const targetDate = payload.date ? String(payload.date).slice(0, 10) : null;

    // إذا تم تمرير شحنات توصيل أو سائق، نجمع الفواتير والطلبيات المرتبطة بها تلقائياً
    if (shipmentIds.length > 0 || driverId) {
      const shConds = ['deleted_at IS NULL'];
      const shParams = [];
      if (shipmentIds.length > 0) {
        shConds.push(`id IN (${shipmentIds.map(() => '?').join(',')})`);
        shParams.push(...shipmentIds);
      }
      if (driverId) {
        shConds.push('driver_id = ?');
        shParams.push(driverId);
      }
      if (targetDate) {
        shConds.push('date(delivery_date) = date(?)');
        shParams.push(targetDate);
      }
      const shipRows = await db.dbAll(`SELECT sale_id, order_id FROM delivery_shipments WHERE ${shConds.join(' AND ')}`, shParams);
      shipRows.forEach((sr) => {
        if (sr.sale_id && !saleIds.includes(Number(sr.sale_id))) saleIds.push(Number(sr.sale_id));
        if (sr.order_id && !orderIds.includes(Number(sr.order_id))) orderIds.push(Number(sr.order_id));
      });
    }

    // إذا لم يُحدد شيء، نأخذ فواتير وطلبيات اليوم
    if (saleIds.length === 0 && orderIds.length === 0) {
      const todayStr = targetDate || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      const todaySales = await db.dbAll(
        'SELECT id FROM sales WHERE date(sale_date) = date(?) AND deleted_at IS NULL AND total > 0 LIMIT 100',
        [todayStr]
      );
      todaySales.forEach((s) => saleIds.push(Number(s.id)));
    }

    const aggregatedMap = {};
    let invoicesCount = saleIds.length + orderIds.length;
    let grandTotalValue = 0;
    let grandTotalWeightKg = 0;

    if (saleIds.length > 0) {
      const ph = saleIds.map(() => '?').join(',');
      const sItems = await db.dbAll(
        `SELECT si.*, p.name AS prod_name, p.barcode, p.oem_reference, p.unit,
                p.wholesale_unit, p.wholesale_qty, p.weight_kg_per_unit
         FROM sale_items si
         LEFT JOIN products p ON p.id = si.product_id
         WHERE si.sale_id IN (${ph}) AND si.deleted_at IS NULL`,
        saleIds
      );

      for (const it of sItems) {
        const key = it.product_id ? `P_${it.product_id}` : `C_${it.custom_product_name || 'item'}`;
        const paidQty = Number(it.quantity || 0);
        const bonusQty = Number(it.bonus_qty || 0);
        const totalUnits = Number((paidQty + bonusQty).toFixed(3));
        const sub = Number(it.subtotal || 0);
        const packMult = Math.max(1, Number(it.pack_multiplier || it.wholesale_qty || 1));
        const unitWeight = Math.max(0, Number(it.weight_kg_per_unit || 0));

        if (!aggregatedMap[key]) {
          aggregatedMap[key] = {
            product_id: it.product_id || null,
            barcode: it.barcode || '',
            oem_reference: it.oem_reference || '',
            name: it.prod_name || it.custom_product_name || 'منتج',
            unit: it.unit || 'قطعة',
            pack_unit: it.wholesale_unit || 'كرتون',
            pack_multiplier: packMult,
            paid_qty: 0,
            bonus_qty: 0,
            total_units: 0,
            full_cartons: 0,
            loose_units: 0,
            total_weight_kg: 0,
            total_value: 0
          };
        }
        const rec = aggregatedMap[key];
        rec.paid_qty = Number((rec.paid_qty + paidQty).toFixed(3));
        rec.bonus_qty = Number((rec.bonus_qty + bonusQty).toFixed(3));
        rec.total_units = Number((rec.total_units + totalUnits).toFixed(3));
        rec.total_value = Number((rec.total_value + sub).toFixed(2));
        rec.total_weight_kg = Number((rec.total_weight_kg + totalUnits * unitWeight).toFixed(2));
        grandTotalValue = Number((grandTotalValue + sub).toFixed(2));
        grandTotalWeightKg = Number((grandTotalWeightKg + totalUnits * unitWeight).toFixed(2));
      }
    }

    if (orderIds.length > 0) {
      const phO = orderIds.map(() => '?').join(',');
      const oItems = await db.dbAll(
        `SELECT oi.*, p.name AS prod_name, p.barcode, p.oem_reference, p.unit,
                p.wholesale_unit, p.wholesale_qty, p.weight_kg_per_unit
         FROM customer_order_items oi
         LEFT JOIN products p ON p.id = oi.product_id
         WHERE oi.order_id IN (${phO}) AND oi.deleted_at IS NULL`,
        orderIds
      );

      for (const it of oItems) {
        const key = it.product_id ? `P_${it.product_id}` : `C_${it.custom_product_name || 'item'}`;
        const totalUnits = Number(it.quantity || 0);
        const sub = Number(it.subtotal || 0);
        const packMult = Math.max(1, Number(it.wholesale_qty || 1));
        const unitWeight = Math.max(0, Number(it.weight_kg_per_unit || 0));

        if (!aggregatedMap[key]) {
          aggregatedMap[key] = {
            product_id: it.product_id || null,
            barcode: it.barcode || '',
            oem_reference: it.oem_reference || '',
            name: it.prod_name || it.custom_product_name || 'منتج',
            unit: it.unit || 'قطعة',
            pack_unit: it.wholesale_unit || 'كرتون',
            pack_multiplier: packMult,
            paid_qty: 0,
            bonus_qty: 0,
            total_units: 0,
            full_cartons: 0,
            loose_units: 0,
            total_weight_kg: 0,
            total_value: 0
          };
        }
        const rec = aggregatedMap[key];
        rec.paid_qty = Number((rec.paid_qty + totalUnits).toFixed(3));
        rec.total_units = Number((rec.total_units + totalUnits).toFixed(3));
        rec.total_value = Number((rec.total_value + sub).toFixed(2));
        rec.total_weight_kg = Number((rec.total_weight_kg + totalUnits * unitWeight).toFixed(2));
        grandTotalValue = Number((grandTotalValue + sub).toFixed(2));
        grandTotalWeightKg = Number((grandTotalWeightKg + totalUnits * unitWeight).toFixed(2));
      }
    }

    const itemsList = Object.values(aggregatedMap).map((r) => {
      if (r.pack_multiplier > 1) {
        r.full_cartons = Math.floor(r.total_units / r.pack_multiplier);
        r.loose_units = Number((r.total_units % r.pack_multiplier).toFixed(3));
      } else {
        r.full_cartons = 0;
        r.loose_units = r.total_units;
      }
      return r;
    });

    let driverInfo = null;
    if (driverId) {
      driverInfo = await db.dbGet('SELECT * FROM drivers WHERE id = ?', [driverId]);
    }

    return {
      manifest_number: `CHG-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${Date.now().toString().slice(-4)}`,
      date: targetDate || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      driver: driverInfo,
      documents_count: invoicesCount,
      items: itemsList,
      total_cartons: itemsList.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.full_cartons || 0), 0),
      total_units: itemsList.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.total_units || 0), 0),
      total_weight_kg: grandTotalWeightKg,
      total_value: grandTotalValue
    };
  }

  return {
    getCustomerLastPrices,
    getReturnableContainers,
    saveReturnableContainer,
    settleReturnableContainer,
    generateTruckLoadingSheet,
    getInstallmentContracts,
    createInstallmentContract,
    payInstallmentSchedule,
    reconcileCcpStatement,
    getCcpReconciliationLogs,
    getEtat104,
    applyLandedCostAndWacToPurchase,
    getWarehouses,
    saveWarehouse,
    deleteWarehouse,
    getStockTransfers,
    createStockTransfer,
    updateBatchShelfLocation,
    getDrivers,
    saveDriver,
    deleteDriver,
    getDeliveryShipments,
    saveDeliveryShipment,
    settleShipmentCod,
    getDiscountCoupons,
    saveDiscountCoupon,
    deleteDiscountCoupon,
    validateDiscountCoupon,
    consumeDiscountCoupon,
    getSystemAuditLogs,
    logSystemAudit,
    getEmployees,
    saveEmployee,
    deleteEmployee,
    getPayrollPayments,
    payEmployeeSalary,
    getServiceTickets,
    saveServiceTicket,
    convertServiceTicketToSale
  };
}

function registerEnterpriseIpcHandlers(ipcMain, db, isLanClientMode, lanClient) {
  const ops = createEnterpriseOperations(db);

  const channels = [
    ['get-installment-contracts', (f) => ops.getInstallmentContracts(f), (f) => lanClient.getInstallmentContracts(f)],
    ['create-installment-contract', (p) => ops.createInstallmentContract(p), (p) => lanClient.createInstallmentContract(p)],
    ['pay-installment-schedule', (p) => ops.payInstallmentSchedule(p), (p) => lanClient.payInstallmentSchedule(p)],
    ['reconcile-ccp-statement', (p) => ops.reconcileCcpStatement(p), (p) => lanClient.reconcileCcpStatement(p)],
    ['get-ccp-reconciliation-logs', (f) => ops.getCcpReconciliationLogs(f), (f) => lanClient.getCcpReconciliationLogs(f)],
    ['get-etat-104', (y) => ops.getEtat104(y), (y) => lanClient.getEtat104(y)],
    ['apply-landed-cost-purchase', (p) => ops.applyLandedCostAndWacToPurchase(p.invoiceId, p), () => ({ ok: true })],
    ['get-warehouses', () => ops.getWarehouses(), () => lanClient.getWarehouses()],
    ['save-warehouse', (p) => ops.saveWarehouse(p), (p) => lanClient.saveWarehouse(p)],
    ['delete-warehouse', (id) => ops.deleteWarehouse(id), (id) => lanClient.deleteWarehouse(id)],
    ['get-stock-transfers', () => ops.getStockTransfers(), () => lanClient.getStockTransfers()],
    ['create-stock-transfer', (p) => ops.createStockTransfer(p), (p) => lanClient.createStockTransfer(p)],
    ['update-batch-shelf', (p) => ops.updateBatchShelfLocation(p), (p) => lanClient.updateBatchShelfLocation(p)],
    ['get-drivers', () => ops.getDrivers(), () => lanClient.getDrivers()],
    ['save-driver', (p) => ops.saveDriver(p), (p) => lanClient.saveDriver(p)],
    ['delete-driver', (id) => ops.deleteDriver(id), (id) => lanClient.deleteDriver(id)],
    ['get-delivery-shipments', (f) => ops.getDeliveryShipments(f), (f) => lanClient.getDeliveryShipments(f)],
    ['save-delivery-shipment', (p) => ops.saveDeliveryShipment(p), (p) => lanClient.saveDeliveryShipment(p)],
    ['settle-shipment-cod', (p) => ops.settleShipmentCod(p), (p) => lanClient.settleShipmentCod(p)],
    ['get-discount-coupons', () => ops.getDiscountCoupons(), () => lanClient.getDiscountCoupons()],
    ['save-discount-coupon', (p) => ops.saveDiscountCoupon(p), (p) => lanClient.saveDiscountCoupon(p)],
    ['delete-discount-coupon', (id) => ops.deleteDiscountCoupon(id), (id) => lanClient.deleteDiscountCoupon(id)],
    ['validate-discount-coupon', (p) => ops.validateDiscountCoupon(p), (p) => lanClient.validateDiscountCoupon(p)],
    ['consume-discount-coupon', (p) => ops.consumeDiscountCoupon(p), () => ({ ok: true })],
    ['get-system-audit-logs', (f) => ops.getSystemAuditLogs(f), (f) => lanClient.getSystemAuditLogs(f)],
    ['log-system-audit', (p) => ops.logSystemAudit(p), (p) => lanClient.logSystemAudit(p)],
    ['get-employees', () => ops.getEmployees(), () => lanClient.getEmployees()],
    ['save-employee', (p) => ops.saveEmployee(p), (p) => lanClient.saveEmployee(p)],
    ['delete-employee', (id) => ops.deleteEmployee(id), (id) => lanClient.deleteEmployee(id)],
    ['get-payroll-payments', () => ops.getPayrollPayments(), () => lanClient.getPayrollPayments()],
    ['pay-employee-salary', (p) => ops.payEmployeeSalary(p), (p) => lanClient.payEmployeeSalary(p)],
    ['get-service-tickets', (f) => ops.getServiceTickets(f), (f) => lanClient.getServiceTickets(f)],
